function readCookie(name) {
    var nameEQ = name + "=";
    var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
    for ( var i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
        var c = ca[i];
        while (c.charAt(0) == ' ')
            c = c.substring(1, c.length);
        if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) == 0)
            return c.substring(nameEQ.length, c.length);
    }
    return null;
}

var pl_did = readCookie("pl_did");
var pl_p = localStorage.pl;

#ot-sdk-btn.ot-sdk-show-settings,

  border: none;
  background: none;
  display: inline;
  padding: 0;
  color: #000;
  font-size: inherit;
}
.ot-fade-in {
  animation-name: none !important;
  animation-duration: initial !important;
  animation-timing-function: initial !important;
}

#onetrust-banner-sdk #onetrust-group-container {
    overflow-y: auto !important;
    overflow-x: hidden !important;
}
@media screen and (max-width: 424px) {
  #onetrust-banner-sdk #onetrust-group-container {
    max-height: calc(100vh - 280px) !important;
  }
}
@media screen and (min-width: 425px) {
  #onetrust-banner-sdk #onetrust-group-container {

                    }
                    lastScrollTop = top <= 0 ? 0 : top;
                },false);
            });
        </script>

                <div class="ddef_h"><span class="def-info ddef-info">  </span><div class="def ddef_d db">If you nail something <a class="query" href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/shut" title="shut" rel="">shut</a>, you put nails in it to <a class="query" href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/fasten" title="fasten" rel="">fasten</a> it so that it cannot <a class="query" href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/easily" title="easily" rel="">easily</a> be <a class="query" href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/open" title="opened" rel="">opened</a>: </div> </div><div class="def-body ddef_b"><div class="examp dexamp"><span class="eg deg">He nailed the <a class="query" href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/box" title="box" rel="">box</a> <a class="query" href="https://dictionary.cambridge.org/dictionary/english/shut" title="shut" rel="">shut</a>.</span></div> </div></div><div class="def-block ddef_block " data-wl-senseid="CACD_00011706_06">

<script type="text/javascript">
    JSON.parse("{&quot;science_geographic_locales/animal-and-plant-biology&quot;:&quot;fit-and-healthy&quot;,&quot;business_financial_industrial_technology/cutting-and-joining&quot;:&quot;fastening-and-tying&quot;,&quot;people_society_religion/honesty-and-dishonesty&quot;:&quot;honesty-openness-and-sincerity&quot;,&quot;people_society_religion/beliefs-and-opinions&quot;:&quot;decisions-and-deciding&quot;,&quot;people_society_religion/communication&quot;:&quot;questioning-people-and-asking-questions-in-general&quot;,&quot;arts_entertainment_media/knowing-and-understanding&quot;:&quot;understanding-and-comprehending&quot;,&quot;arts_entertainment_media/chance-and-possibility&quot;:&quot;certainty&quot;,&quot;arts_entertainment_media/chance-and-possibility&quot;:&quot;impossible-and-improbable&quot;,&quot;shopping_consumer_resources/household-equipment&quot;:&quot;nuts-bolts-and-screws&quot;,&quot;beauty_health_fitness/animal-anatomy&quot;:&quot;the-foot&quot;,&quot;beauty_health_fitness/animal-anatomy&quot;:&quot;the-hand&quot;,&quot;jobs_education_resumes/doing-and-achieving&quot;:&quot;succeeding-achieving-and-fulfilling&quot;,&quot;jobs_education_resumes/doing-and-achieving&quot;:&quot;failures&quot;,&quot;jobs_education_resumes/expressing-opposition-and-disapproval&quot;:&quot;blaming-and-accusing&quot;,&quot;jobs_education_resumes/communication&quot;:&quot;defining-and-explaining&quot;,&quot;law_government_military/right-and-wrong&quot;:&quot;proving-and-disproving&quot;,&quot;law_government_military/breaking-and-damaging&quot;:&quot;hitting-and-beating&quot;,&quot;law_government_military/law-and-the-police&quot;:&quot;arresting-and-charging&quot;}".replace(/(&quot\;)/g,"\""), (chanel, topic) => {
        if(typeof topic === 'string') {
            sendGAEvent('Channelization', chanel, topic, {'nonInteraction':1});
            // alert(chanel +','+ topic);
        }
    });
</script>

  <script type="text/javascript">
	var polarbyteConfig = {
		appId: "cdo",
        anonymousTrackingMode: "full"
        	}
            	polarbyteConfig.pageContext = {
    	locale:   "en" ,
        section:  "dictionary" ,
        subsection:  "english" ,
        type:  "english_entry"         }
    
              polarbyteConfig.productContext = {"id":"english/nail","type":"MonoEngEntry"};
    
    		polarbyteConfig.entryContext = {"entry_id":"nail","name":"nail","datasets":["english"],"domain":"RDAK","language":["en"],"levels":["B2"],"pos":["noun"],"region":null,"usage":null}
    
    		polarbyteConfig.searchMetadata = {"dataset":"English","datasetType":"english","searchFormId":"searchForm","searchFieldId":"searchword"}
    
    
                polarbyteConfig.survey = {"apiHost":"https://cloud.polarbyte.com/webapps-api","enabled":true,"projectName":"CDO","userAttributes":{"country":"TW"},"contexts":{"page":{"locale":true,"section":true,"subsection":true,"type":true,"scenario":false}}}
    
    
    window.assistantTracker = {
        sendEvent: function(event, metadata) {
            if (!window || !window.polarbytereach || !window.polarbytereach.trackPublisherCustomEvent) {
                console.error('Failed to call polarbytereach.trackPublisherCustomEvent, tracker is not available:', { event, metadata});
                return;
            }

            try {
                window.polarbytereach.trackPublisherCustomEvent(event, metadata);
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Failed to call polarbytereach.trackPublisherCustomEvent:', { error, event, metadata });
            }
        },

        getEvent: function(action, label, property, value) {
            return {category: 'AI_ASSISTANT', action: action, label: label, property: property, value: value};
        },

        eventHeaderBtnClick: function() {
            var event = this.getEvent('HEADER_BTN_CLICK');
            this.sendEvent(event, undefined);
        },

        eventBurgerMenuItemClick: function() {
            var event = this.getEvent('BURGER_MENU_ITEM_CLICK');
            this.sendEvent(event, undefined);
        },

        eventMobileStickyBtnClick: function() {
            var event = this.getEvent('MOBILE_STICKY_BTN_CLICK');
            this.sendEvent(event, undefined);
        }
    };
  </script>

                                                        <span data-dictcode="essential-american-english" class="hp hdb hp" on="tap:AMP.setState({ stateSearch: { dataset: 'essential-american-english', dataset_text: 'Essential American English', dataset_search: 'Search Essential American English', isbilang: false } }), sidebarDict.close, searchword.focus" role="button" aria-label="Set dictionary search to Essential American English" tabindex="0" title="Essential American English">Essential American English</span>
